[concept] identity: A dire wolf whose hide is sheathed in hoarfrost, it circles the pack's prey at a loping trot before lunging in to hamstring and drag down, then peels away before the quarry can answer. It fights as part of the pack, driving prey toward its fellows and biting at flanks and hocks, and it never stands and trades blows. When bloodied it does not break and run — it retreats just far enough to let the cold of its pelt seep into the wound before wheeling back in for another pass, fighting to the kill as long as the pack holds.
[concept] lore: Something in the high passes killed a wolf and left it in the snow too long, and the winter kept it; the frost-rimed dire wolf now runs with whatever pack will have it, hunting warmth it can no longer feel.

Traits

Amphibious. The leviathan can breathe air and water.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the leviathan fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The leviathan makes two Bite attacks.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage plus 14 (4d6) Thunder damage.

Crushing Roar (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature in a 60-foot Cone. Failure: 78 (12d12) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Bonus Actions

Retaliating Thunderclap. One creature within 5 feet that hit the leviathan with an attack roll since the end of the leviathan's last turn takes 14 (4d6) Thunder damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the leviathan can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Surging Lunge. The leviathan moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Bite attack.

Tide Slam. Strength Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature within 10 feet of the leviathan. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: A Gargantuan leviathan of the lightless trench, it opens a fleet-fight by hanging in the deep and loosing its drowning song — a crushing subsonic drone that shudders hulls and drags sailors down — then rises to smash ships with tidal mass and coils of brine-heavy flesh. Once bloodied it does not flee; it redoubles the song until the water around it is a killing pressure, crushing and drowning everything that floats or swims, and it will circle a broken fleet for hours rather than let a single survivor go.
[concept] lore: It is a trench-born monstrosity grown vast on the drowned dead of a thousand wrecks, and it sings to pull new hulls down into the cold dark where it can feed and nest.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

STR +3

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 12

Languages:

Challenge

1 (200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Mire Lurker. While the hound remains motionless, it is indistinguishable from a patch of soggy bank.
Whelm. As a bonus action, the hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the hound's turns. The hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the hound. If the hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The hound makes two Bite attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Mire Drag.

Mire Drag: One creature within 20 feet of the hound must succeed on a DC 13 Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 15 feet straight toward the hound by its jaws and be grappled (escape DC 11).

[concept] identity: The archon opens by loosing a churning column of flies from its hollow ribcage, a living veil that blinds and poisons before it ever closes to strike. Every round thereafter it dives through the swarm on wings of clotted light, raking with pestilent talons and snatching the weakened skyward to drop them, while its commands ripple through the lesser flies to reposition and harry. [concept] lore: Once a radiant archon of the upper courts, it was condemned to carry a plague it could not cure and now drifts between mortal battlefields, seeding pestilence where it once delivered mercy.

Adult Red Dragon
Huge dragon, chaotic evil
Armor Class

19 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

256 (19d12 + 133)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


27 (+8)

	

10 (+0)

	

25 (+7)

	

16 (+3)

	

13 (+1)

	

23 (+6)

fire

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

17 (18,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the red dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The red dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The red dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The red dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The red dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The red dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

Cold Absorption. Whenever the glacier's heart is subjected to cold damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of hit points equal to the cold damage dealt.
Siege Monster. The glacier's heart deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions
Multiattack: The glacier's heart makes two Slam attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Heartchill Lash.
Slam: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 22 (4d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) cold damage.
Killing Cold (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) cold damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also poisoned until the end of its next turn.
Heartchill Lash: One creature within 30 feet that is poisoned must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 26 (4d12) cold damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.

Huge Construct, Lawful Evil

AC 19
Initiative +6 (16)

Immunities Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 20

Languages Common

CR 17 (XP 18,000; PB +6)

Traits

Seam Scorch. A creature that touches the tyrant or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) Fire damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Forge Stride. The tyrant ignores Difficult Terrain, and Opportunity Attacks against it are made with Disadvantage.

Furnace Pivot. When a creature the tyrant can see ends its turn within 5 feet of it, the tyrant can move up to 15 feet without provoking Opportunity Attacks.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes three attacks, using Brass Fist or Furnace Vent in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Brass Backlash.

Brass Fist. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 15 (2d8 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 10 (3d6) Fire damage.

Furnace Vent. Ranged Attack Roll: +12, range 60/240 ft. 20 (4d6 + 6) Fire damage.

Forge Breath (Recharge 5-6). Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 60-foot-long, 10-foot-wide Line. Failure: 72 (16d8) Fire damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Brass Backlash. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 19, one creature within 30 feet that is Frightened of the tyrant. Failure: 21 (6d6) Fire damage. Success: Half damage.

Bonus Actions

Fleet Agility. The tyrant takes the Dash or Disengage action.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Sledge Advance. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Brass Fist attack.

Vent Scorch. The tyrant makes one Furnace Vent attack. The tyrant can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.

Riposte. Trigger: A creature within 5 feet of Vashka misses it with a melee attack roll. Response: Vashka makes one Warhammer attack against that creature.

[concept] identity: A convocation of tarnished funeral bells drifts on no wind, ringing in overlapping peals that swell into a deafening knell as it settles over the dying. Every round it hovers above its chosen prey, sounding the death-rite until the target's own wounds answer the tolling, then surges into a single clangorous mass to strike with its iron lip and clapper. It does not flee when bloodied — it splits into two lesser peals and redoubles the ringing, patient and ceremonial, waiting out the last breath.
[concept] lore: These bells were looted from a hundred drowned chapel towers and steeple-caught graves, and they gather wherever death is slow, wanting to be rung over a final breath they were denied.

[concept] identity: The smoking hydra is a brute that wades into the thick of a fight with a wall of snapping heads, trusting its reknitting flesh to soak the first blows while it seizes and tears. Every wound it takes seals shut in a hiss of hot smoke, and every head it loses leaves a blackened, smouldering stump rather than a fresh neck — so it fights all the more savagely as its own body dims and chokes, battering prey with bloodied jaws and suffocating them in the fumes rising from its cauterised wounds. Cornered or half-consumed, it does not retreat; it presses the assault, snarling through the smoke, as though the fire in its stumps is all that is left of its rage.
[concept] lore: A hydra that swallowed a dying ember-drake's heart still heals every wound — but its severed necks no longer regrow flesh, only smoulder, so it seeks to bury its burning stumps in living prey before the smoke eats it whole.

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 12

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 8 (XP 3,900; PB +3)

Traits

Immutable Form. The golem can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The golem has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Acid Absorption. Whenever the golem is subjected to Acid damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of Hit Points equal to the Acid damage dealt.

Berserk. Whenever the golem starts its turn Bloodied, roll 1d6. On a 6, the golem goes berserk. On each of its turns while berserk, the golem attacks the nearest creature it can see. If no creature is near enough to move to and attack, the golem attacks an object. Once the golem goes berserk, it continues to be berserk until it is destroyed or it is no longer Bloodied.

Actions

Multiattack. The golem makes two Slam attacks, or it makes three Slam attacks if it used Hasten this turn.

Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 10 (1d10 + 5) Bludgeoning damage plus 6 (1d12) Acid damage, and the target's Hit Point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the Acid damage taken.

Timber Trample (Recharge 5-6). The golem moves up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks and can move through the spaces of Large or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters is targeted once by the following effect. Strength Saving Throw: DC 16. Failure: 27 (6d8) Bludgeoning damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Bonus Actions

Hasten (Recharge 5-6). The golem takes the Dash and Disengage actions.

CR 10 (XP 5,900; PB +4)

Traits

Stone Adhesion. The golem adheres to anything that touches it. A Huge or smaller creature adhered to the golem is also Grappled by it (escape DC 18). Ability checks made to escape this grapple have Disadvantage.

Immutable Form. The golem can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The golem has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The golem makes two attacks, using Slam or Force Bolt in any combination.

Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 15 (2d8 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 9 (2d8) Force damage.

Force Bolt. Ranged Attack Roll: +9, range 120 ft. 22 (4d10) Force damage.

Bonus Actions

Slow (Recharge 5-6). The golem casts the Slow spell, requiring no spell components and using Constitution as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17).

Drowned Choir
Large undead, neutral evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

178 (21d10 + 63)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


14 (+2)

17 (+3)

Perception +6, Deception +7

WIS +6, CHA +7

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 16

Common plus the languages it knew in life

Challenge

10 (5,900 XP)

+4

Swelling Hymn. The first time the drowned choir has half of its hit points or fewer in a fight, it releases a burst. Each creature within 20 feet of the drowned choir must make a DC 15 Constitution saving throw, taking 21 (6d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also deafened until the end of its next turn.
Captain's Charm. As a bonus action, the drowned choir targets one creature it can see within 30 feet of it. That creature must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be charmed by the drowned choir until the start of the drowned choir's next turn.

Actions
Multiattack: The drowned choir makes three attacks, using Chorus or Hymnal Bolt. It can replace one attack with a use of Descant.
Chorus: Melee Spell Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (4d8 + 3) necrotic damage.
Hymnal Bolt: Ranged Spell Attack: +7 to hit, range 80/320 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) necrotic damage.
Descant: One creature within 60 feet must make a DC 15 Constitution saving throw, taking 18 (4d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. On a failed save, the target is also deafened until the end of its next turn.
Spellcasting: The drowned choir casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 15): -

At will: detect magic, mage hand

1/day each: fear, hypnotic pattern
Creeping Dread: One creature within 60 feet must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened of the drowned choir until the end of its next turn. If the target was already frightened by this ability, it also takes 21 (6d6) necrotic damage.
```

Each round thereafter it layers harmonies that steal actions — a verse to stun, a descant to lure a listener closer, a low pedal tone to pin a foe in place — while drifting through flooded nave and nave as a single vast corpse-voice. It does not flee; wounded, it simply sings louder, gathering the drowned faithful in its chorus until nothing near can hear its own thoughts.
[concept] lore: When the cathedral sank beneath the flood, the choir kept singing to hold back the dark water — and the dark water kept them, so now the drowned congregation sings in one borrowed voice, dragging the living down to join the verse.

Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

Damage Immunities:

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Legendary Resistance. If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., Fly Speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Vitality Leech.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of two hands.
Vitality Leech: One creature within 30 feet of the vampire must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 38 (11d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. The vampire regains hit points equal to half the damage dealt.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet of the vampire that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A Humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a Vampire Spawn under the vampire's control.
```

Purple Worm

Gargantuan Monstrosity, Unaligned

AC 18
Initiative -2 (8)

Speed 50 ft., Burrow 50 ft.

Skills Athletics +14, Perception +4

Senses blindsight 30 ft., tremorsense 60 ft.; Passive Perception 14

Languages None

CR 15 (XP 13,000; PB +5)

Traits

Tunneler. The purple worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The purple worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack. It can replace one attack with a use of Engulf.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Engulf. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature within 10 feet that the purple worm hit with a melee attack this turn. Failure: 42 (12d6) Piercing damage, and the target is swallowed. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the purple worm, and takes 22 (5d8) Acid damage at the start of each of the purple worm's turns. If the purple worm takes 40 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, it must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 10 feet of the purple worm and has the Prone condition. If the purple worm dies, a swallowed creature is no longer Restrained and can escape from the corpse using 15 feet of movement, exiting with the Prone condition.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature Grappled by the purple worm (it can have up to three creatures swallowed at a time). Failure: The target is swallowed by the purple worm, and the Grappled condition ends. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the purple worm, and takes 17 (5d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the purple worm's turns.
If the purple worm takes 30 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, the purple worm must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 5 feet of the purple worm and has the Prone condition. If the purple worm dies, any swallowed creature no longer has the Restrained condition and can escape from the corpse using 20 feet of movement, exiting Prone.

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Fiery Mace. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (4d6 + 8) Force damage plus 21 (6d6) Fire damage.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

Reactions

Riposte. Trigger: A creature within 5 feet of the fiend misses it with a melee attack roll. Response: The fiend makes one Bite attack against that creature.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the heliarch fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, the heliarch reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the heliarch's lair.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When the heliarch is hit by an attack, the heliarch casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: The heliarch makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Bestow Curse.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target is paralyzed until the start of the heliarch's next turn.
Spellcasting: The heliarch casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Legendary Actions

The heliarch can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The heliarch regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: The heliarch teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of the heliarch must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. Whether it succeeds or fails, the heliarch can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: The heliarch casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. The heliarch can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

When finally bloodied it does not flee — it sets the whole ossuary spinning to fling lesser bones free and fight to the last, coldly certain that the dead have nothing left to lose.
[concept] lore: An archlich who refused the grave of dust and instead spent its final centuries welding the bones of a fallen age into a small sun around its throne, so that it might watch over the still-living dead from a height undying eyes cannot reach.

Amphibious. The gold dragon can breathe air and water.

Actions
Multiattack: The gold dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Spellcasting to cast Guiding Bolt (level 4 version) or (B) Weakening Breath.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) slashing damage plus 9 (2d8) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The gold dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 24, +16 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: detect magic, guiding bolt, shapechange

1/day each: flame strike, word of recall, zone of truth
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 90-foot cone must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: Each creature that isn't currently affected by this breath in a 90-foot cone must make a DC 24 Strength saving throw. On a failed save, the target has disadvantage on Strength-based D20 tests and subtracts 5 (1d10) from its damage rolls. It repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.
Legendary Actions

The gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Banish: One creature the gold dragon can see within 120 feet of it must make a DC 24 Charisma saving throw. On a failed save, it takes 24 (7d6) force damage, and it is incapacitated and transported to a harmless demiplane until the start of the gold dragon's next turn, at which point it reappears in an unoccupied space of the gold dragon's choice within 120 feet of the gold dragon. Whether it succeeds or fails, the gold dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Guiding Light: The gold dragon uses Spellcasting to cast guiding bolt (cast at 4th level).
Pounce: The gold dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.
```

Actions

Multiattack. The broodling makes two attacks, using Scythe-Arm or Carving Scythe-Arm in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Withering Dread.

Scythe-Arm. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 18 (4d6 + 4) Slashing damage plus 13 (3d8) Acid damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Withering Dread. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of the broodling's next turn. If the target was already Frightened by this ability, it also takes 32 (5d12) Acid damage.

Carving Scythe-Arm. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 18 (4d6 + 4) Slashing damage.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. The broodling swallows one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While swallowed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the broodling, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the broodling's turns. The broodling can have only one creature swallowed at a time. If the broodling takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the swallowed creature, the broodling must regurgitate the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the broodling. If the broodling dies, a swallowed creature exits the body Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

CR 7 (XP 2,900; PB +3)

Actions

Multiattack. The hagsight makes three attacks, using Rusted Blade or Withering Bolt in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Mire Sap.

Rusted Blade. Melee Attack Roll: +7, reach 5 ft. 17 (3d8 + 4) Slashing damage.

Withering Bolt. Ranged Attack Roll: +7, range 80/320 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Piercing damage.

Mire Sap. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: 14 (4d6) Poison damage, and the target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn.

Spellcasting. The hagsight casts one of the following spells, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 15):

- At Will: Fog Cloud
- 1/Day Each: Bestow Curse, Slow

Bog-Bind Touch. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: 17 (5d6) Necrotic damage, and the target can't regain Hit Points until the end of the hagsight's next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Trespasser's Curse. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: 19 (3d12) Necrotic damage, and the target has the Charmed condition until the end of its next turn.

Reactions

Protective Magic (3/Day). The hagsight casts Counterspell or Shield in response to the spell's trigger, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Medium ooze, unaligned

157 (21d8 + 63)

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

+4

Reliquary Digestion. When the vault ooze is in contact with a reliquary or similar consecrated object, the vault ooze corrodes the object, destroying it after is dealt to it, and regains 14 (4d6) hit points.
Aberrant Ground. The ground in a 10-foot-radius sphere centered on the vault ooze is difficult terrain.
Amorphous. The vault ooze can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch wide without squeezing.
Corrosive Form. A creature that touches the vault ooze or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 9 (2d8) acid damage.
Spider Climb. The vault ooze can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.

Actions
Multiattack: The vault ooze makes two Pseudopod attacks.
Pseudopod: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 27 (7d6 + 3) acid damage. Nonmagical armor worn by the target takes a -1 penalty to the AC it offers. The armor is destroyed if the penalty reduces its AC to 10. The penalty can be removed by casting the mending spell on the armor.

[concept] identity: The thing in the well waits at the bottom of its stone shaft as a half-submerged mass of wet gray hide, near-indistinguishable from a rotting bucket or a drowned dog until a face leans over the lip — then it erupts up the well's throat with a burbling shriek, seizes the rim with boneless finger-limbs, and drags itself and its prey down together. Once it has a grip it does not let go: it wraps, crushes and coils, crushing breath out of a body in water-choked spasms, and it never climbs out after a meal that escapes — but anything that wounds it deeply learns the shaft has no bottom and it simply sinks, dragging whatever it has hold of with it. It is patient, gluttonous, and utterly certain that anything above the water is food.
[concept] lore: Something drowned in this well and refused to finish drowning, and now it grows fat on whatever comes to draw water or gawk down the shaft — it wants only to keep its water high and its belly full.

-3

Skills Athletics +6, Perception +2

Resistances Cold

Immunities Poison; Exhaustion, Poisoned

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 12

Languages None

CR 4 (XP 1,100; PB +2)

Traits

Silt Burial. While in silt, mud, or similar loose ground, the sludgeshell priest can spend 1 minute to bury itself in the silt, gaining Total Cover against creatures outside the silt until it moves or attacks.

Faultless Tracker. The sludgeshell priest knows the direction and distance to any creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, as long as the two of them are on the same plane of existence.

Actions

Multiattack. The sludgeshell priest makes two Crushing Claw attacks.

Crushing Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d8 + 4) Slashing damage plus 6 (1d12) Cold damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14). While Grappled, the target has the Restrained condition.

Rime Shard Volley (Recharge 5-6). Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 15-foot-radius Sphere centered on a point within 60 feet. Failure: 22 (5d8) Cold damage. Success: Half damage.

Bonus Actions

Entomb. The sludgeshell priest entombs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While entombed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the sludgeshell priest, and takes 6 (1d12) Cold damage at the start of each of the sludgeshell priest's turns. The sludgeshell priest can have only one creature entombed at a time. If the sludgeshell priest takes 12 damage or more in a single turn from the entombed creature, the sludgeshell priest must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the sludgeshell priest. If the sludgeshell priest dies, an entombed creature breaks free Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

It buries itself beneath silt and flotsam to wait with the patience of the long-drowned, then lunges from cover to seize prey in its one crushing claw while its other arm hurls shards of frozen temple-stone at victims who flee across open water. When bloodied it simply sinks beneath the surface and skitters back into the dark to freeze and wait for another chance, never fleeing far and never letting a wounded thing leave alive. It is mindless (INT 1-2), and it is a patient killer that strikes from extreme range.

Meshrin
Large humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (chain mail)

Hit Points: 

120 (14d10 + 42)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

Perception +5, Stealth +7

DEX +7, WIS +5

passive Perception 15

Common, Draconic

8 (3,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Wounded Frenzy. While Meshrin has half of its hit points or fewer, it has advantage on attack rolls and saving throws.
Hold Breath. Meshrin can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Great Weapon Master. When Meshrin hits a creature with its greataxe, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost Meshrin extra movement, and Meshrin can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Fleet Agility. As a bonus action, Meshrin takes the Dash or Disengage action.

Actions
Multiattack: Meshrin makes three attacks, using Greataxe or Javelin. It can replace one attack with a use of Downed Strike.
Greataxe: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) slashing damage.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, a javelin returns to Meshrin's grasp.
Downed Strike: One creature within 30 feet that is prone must make a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw, taking 17 (5d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Frightening Roar (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened of Meshrin until the end of its next turn.

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 12

Languages Understands Common plus one other language but can't speak

CR 6 (XP 2,300; PB +3)

Traits

Great Weapon Master. When the skeleton hits a creature with its Greatsword, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The skeleton makes two attacks, using Greatsword or Shortbow in any combination.

Greatsword. Melee Attack Roll: +7, reach 10 ft. 22 (4d8 + 4) Slashing damage.

Shortbow. Ranged Attack Roll: +7, range 80/320 ft. 17 (3d8 + 4) Piercing damage.
